# FrameXML templates

Virtual frames that Blizzard's UI defines. Inherit one with `CreateFrame("Button", nil, parent, "UIPanelButtonTemplate")` or `inherits="..."` in XML. Intrinsic frames are new widget types usable as the first argument of `CreateFrame`.
